However, pseudo-labels have mixed reliability, and a single LoRA adapter must absorb reliable, ambiguous, and noisy gradients in the same low-rank space. This can make VFM adaptation sensitive to pseudo-label noise. We propose \textbf{TriNoL}, a \textbf{Tri}ple-expert learning framework from \textbf{No}isy \textbf{L}abels for semi-supervised VFM adaptation. TriNoL routes unlabeled samples into three confidence regions and assigns them to three LoRA experts: a Positive Expert for high-confidence pseudo-labels, an Alignment Expert for medium-confidence ambiguous samples, and a Negative Expert for low-confidence noisy samples. The VFM backbone remains frozen, and only the LoRA experts and classifier head are updated. By separating different pseudo-label reliability regions into specialized adaptation paths, TriNoL improves robustness to noisy supervision while keeping the training cost low.

</details>


### [165] [Model the Edit, Not the Image: Visual Autoregressive Editing from a Source-Centric Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09057)
*Hongyi Fang,Chuwen Xie,Benjia Zhou,Yu-Xuan Qiu,Chenggong Hu,Zhibin Wang,Chao Chen,Jianbin Qin,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EditMod是一种基于源中心视角的视觉自回归模型（VAR）文本引导图像编辑方法，通过比较源与目标条件下的预测差异作为编辑方向，对源token进行残差更新，在保持高保真度的同时实现快速编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的训练免VAR编辑方法采用生成中心范式，将编辑视为受源图像约束的目标条件再生成，未能充分利用VAR提供的多尺度源表示，且可能引入额外计算或干预。

Method: 采用源中心视角：将编码后的源图像token作为主要视觉状态；比较共享自回归上下文下源条件与目标条件的预测，将两者差异视为逐尺度编辑方向；在选定尺度上以残差更新方式应用到源token。

Result: EditMod在源图像保真度上达到领先水平，同时保持强文本对齐；在单块A100 GPU上完成1K图像的端到端编辑仅需1.57秒，无需逐图像预处理。

Conclusion: 通过将编辑范式从生成中心转向源中心，EditMod更充分地利用了VAR的多尺度源表示，实现了既高效又高质量的文本引导图像编辑。

Abstract: Next-scale visual autoregressive models (VARs) have emerged as a powerful generative paradigm, producing high-quality images through efficient coarse-to-fine prediction. However, their potential for text-guided image editing remains largely underexplored. Existing training-free VAR editing approaches often formulate editing as target-conditioned regeneration guided or constrained by the source image, and may rely on inversion, test-time optimization, attention control, or user-provided masks. This generation-centric formulation does not fully exploit the multiscale source representations provided by VARs and may introduce additional computation or intervention. We instead take a source-centric perspective on VAR editing, in which the encoded source image tokens serve as the primary visual state and the editing process focuses on condition-induced changes. Based on this perspective, we propose \textbf{EditMod}, which compares source- and target-conditioned predictions under a shared autoregressive context, treats their difference as a scale-wise editing direction, and applies it as a residual update to source tokens at selected scales. Experiments show that EditMod achieves leading source-image fidelity while maintaining strong text alignment, and completes end-to-end editing of a 1K image in only 1.57 seconds on a single A100 GPU without per-image preparation.

</details>


### [166] [Learning human joint torques from pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09083)
*Chen Chen,Rui Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VID，一个基于真实单目图像预测人体关节力矩的视觉逆动力学数据集和基准，包含63,369帧同步数据及OpenSim动力学标签，并设计VID-Network模型（结合姿态预训练空间概率特征、标记点回归和时间力矩推理），在整体mPJE达1.7612 N·m/kg，较最优基线提升39.81%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关节力矩估计方法高度依赖表面肌电、动捕标记点、测力台或仿真数据，难以应用于普通RGB图像的真实场景，缺乏从视觉观测直接进行生物力学分析的可用数据集和基准。

Method: 构建VID数据集（含63,369帧真实人体图像、运动学标注、人体测量属性和OpenSim动力学标签），定义统一评估协议（整体力矩、关节特定、动作特定三个层面），并提出VID-Network：融合姿态预训练空间概率特征、标记点回归和时间力矩推断模块，从图像序列恢复关节力矩。

Result: VID-Network在VID上整体mPJE为1.7612 N·m/kg，较最优基线提升39.81%，在所有关节类型和大多数动作类别上均取得最低误差。

Conclusion: VID是首个面向视觉驱动人体逆动力学的实用基准，为在非受控环境中进行生物力学推断奠定了基础，推动了生物力学分析从实验室走向真实场景。

Abstract: Estimating human joint torques from visual observations is a key step toward bringing biomechanical analysis from controlled laboratories to real-world movement scenarios. Existing torque estimation methods typically depend on surface electromyography, motion-capture markers, force plates, or simulated imitation data, which limits their applicability to ordinary RGB images. In this work, we introduce VID, a vision-based inverse dynamics dataset and benchmark for predicting human joint torques directly from real monocular images. VID contains 63,369 synchronized frames with real human images, kinematic annotations, anthropometric attributes, and OpenSim-derived dynamic labels, providing paired visual and biomechanical supervision for real-image inverse dynamics. We further define a standardized evaluation protocol covering overall torque estimation, joint-specific analysis, and action-specific prediction. To establish a strong reference model, we propose VID-Network, which combines pose-pretrained spatial probabilistic features, marker regression, and temporal torque inference to recover joint torques from image sequences. Experiments on VID show that VID-Network achieves an overall mPJE of 1.7612 N$\cdot$m/kg, improving over the best compared baseline by 39.81\%, and obtains the lowest error across all evaluated joint types and most action categories. VID establishes a first practical benchmark for vision-driven human inverse dynamics and provides a foundation for studying biomechanical inference in less constrained environments.

</details>


### [167] [TLDChoiceNet: Quantitatively Choosing a Transfer Learning Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09091)
*Jing Ning,James D. Braza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TLDChoiceNet模型，用于在给定微调数据集时预测不同迁移学习数据集在微调后的测试集准确率，从而选择最优的迁移学习数据集。该模型通过ImageNet预训练ResNet50嵌入实现0.031的MSE（比简单版本低5倍），并设计了分布距离（DD）和平均类相关性（ACC）两个无监督指标来指导数据集选择，ACC的R²达到0.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据有限的场景中，迁移学习尤为重要，但目前缺乏一种定量方法来选择最佳的迁移学习数据集。虽然直觉上认为迁移学习数据集应比微调数据集拥有更多的类别和每类样本数，但没有可靠的量化手段来指导这一选择。

Method: 设计了TLDChoiceNet模型，通过预测微调后的测试集准确率来选择最佳迁移学习数据集。版本1为简单基线，版本2利用ImageNet预训练的ResNet50 v2嵌入并结合每类信息，实现更低的MSE。此外，提出了两个无监督指标：分布距离（DD）和平均类相关性（ACC），用于无监督地选择最优迁移学习数据集。

Result: 版本1的MSE为0.154，版本2的MSE降至0.031（降低5倍）。DD指标对微调准确率的线性回归R²为0.89，ACC指标将R²提升至0.97。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，数据集的低层统计特征能够解释迁移学习的效果，且使用ImageNet预训练模型可以将不同类在潜在特征空间中嵌入得更远，从而提升迁移学习数据集选择的准确性和可解释性。

Abstract: Transfer learning is particularly useful in settings with limited training data, and within image classification it is common to transfer learn upon massive datasets like ImageNet , CIFAR-100, or COCO . Qualitatively, it seems a transfer learning dataset should have both more classes and more examples per class than the fine tuning dataset; however, a quantitative method to choose the best transfer learning dataset does not currently exist. In this paper, we design TLDChoiceNet, a model to choose the best transfer learning dataset given a fine tuning dataset by predicting the test-set accuracy after fine-tuning. A simple version 1 achieves 0.154 MSE on the test dataset, while a version 2 leveraging an ImageNet pre-trained ResNet50 v2 embedding with per-class information attains a 5X lower MSE of 0.031. We further design two metrics that enable an unsupervised method of choosing an optimal transfer learning dataset: distribution distance (DD), which linearly regresses against fine-tune accuracy with an R2 of 0.89, and average class correlation (ACC), which improves the R2 to 0.97. Our results underscore that a dataset's low-level statistics can explain the transfer learning effect, and that using a pre-trained ImageNet can embed different classes further apart in latent feature space.

</details>


### [168] [SI-Edit: Toward Sketch-Instruction Guided Local Image Editing with Pixel-Level Precision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09097)
*Weixin Ye,Wei Wang,Hongguang Zhu,Xuecheng Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对草图引导的图像编辑中像素级精度不足的问题，提出了SI-Data数据集和SI-Edit协作框架。SI-Data通过MLLM自动化管道合成包含原图、局部几何草图、语义指令和编辑结果的四元组；SI-Edit将语义指令与精确几何约束集成，并建立了结构保真度和语义遵循度的评估指标。实验证明SI-Edit在结构控制和像素级局部细化方面优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 草图引导的图像编辑虽然在生成模型上取得进展，但在像素级精度和细粒度局部形变方面仍面临挑战。核心瓶颈在于缺乏同时提供几何约束和语义指令的高质量公开基准数据集。

Method: 1) 提出SI-Data数据集，利用MLLMs自动化管道合成包含原图、局部几何草图、语义指令及编辑后图像的四元组数据；2) 提出SI-Edit协作框架，将语义指令与精确几何约束集成，实现空间-语义协同学习。

Result: SI-Edit在结构控制可靠性方面优于基线方法，能够实现精确的像素级局部细化，并与用户意图保持一致。同时建立了包括草图-边缘对齐等结构保真度指标和语义遵循度指标的综合评估体系。

Conclusion: SI-Data有效缓解了草图编辑基准数据短缺的问题，SI-Edit验证了语义-几何协同学习的有效性，为草图引导的像素级图像编辑提供了新的数据基础和框架，且数据和代码已开源。

Abstract: Despite rapid advances in generative models, achieving pixel-level precision in sketch-based image editing remains a persistent challenge, particularly for fine-grained local deformations. This gap stems primarily from the critical shortage of high-quality, publicly available benchmark datasets that jointly provide geometric constraints and semantic instructions. To address this issue, we first introduce **SI-Data**, a high-quality dataset specifically designed for instruction-guided local sketch editing. We develop an automated pipeline leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to synthesize comprehensive quadruplets comprising original images, local geometric sketches, semantic instructions, and corresponding edited images. By providing both reliable spatial anchors and explicit semantic intent, SI-Data uniquely enables collaborative spatial-semantic learning. Building upon this, we propose a collaborative framework called **SI-Edit** that integrates semantic instructions with precise geometric constraints. Furthermore, to address the lack of standardized evaluation, we establish a comprehensive set of metrics designed to measure both structural fidelity (e.g., sketch-to-edge alignment) and semantic adherence. Experimental results demonstrate that SI-Edit provides more reliable structural control than baselines for sketch-based image editing, and achieves precise, pixel-level local refinements aligned with user intent. The data and code are released on the [project page](https://github.com/ywxsuperstar/SIEdit).

</details>


### [169] [Contrastive Mask Fidelity: Reference-Free Auditing of Ground-Truth Masks in Remote Sensing Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09101)
*Shuaishuai Cao,Shuwei Peng,Meng Tang,Min Huang,Youjin Wang,Jie Chen,Jing Ouyang,Zhiwei Zhai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出对比掩码保真度（CMF），一种无需训练、无需参考的指标，直接基于图像证据对竞态语义分割掩码进行评分。作者在10个遥感基准的10,731个图像-类别对上审计后发现，人造类别（建筑物、道路、车辆）的候选掩码在62-85%的对中优于人工标注，而模糊土地覆盖类别则相反。CMF在81%的样本上与专家判断一致，超过多种基线，并能通过保守的类别级仲裁改进跨域迁移监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感语义分割的标注往往粗糙、不完整或错位，但模型评估仍以人工标注的掩码为基准。这造成评估悖论：高分可能反映对不完美标签的认同，而非对图像内容的忠实。因此需要一个独立于标注的、直接基于图像证据的掩码质量评估指标。

Method: CMF通过构建保留与擦除的反事实视图（keep and erase counterfactual views），使用冻结的视觉-语言模型作为裁判，判断类别证据是否集中掩码内部且外部无此类证据。在10个遥感基准上使用Seg-Probe（一种基于SegEarth-OV3的无需训练的开源词汇探针）生成候选掩码，并对10,731个图像-类别对进行审计。

Result: Seg-Probe在十个数据集的九个上优于此前基线。审计显示系统性、类别依赖的标注失真：人造类别（建筑物、道路、车辆）在62-85%的对中候选掩码更优，而模糊土地覆盖类别更常偏好人工标注。在盲化三标注者共识测试中，CMF在81%对上与专家判断一致，超过keep-only评分、模型置信度和训练的标签质量基线。类别级仲裁可生成改善跨域迁移的监督信号。

Conclusion: CMF可作为可扩展的大规模标注审计工具，挑战了"标注必然正确"的假设，为遥感分割中标签质量审计与监督改善提供了新路径。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation models are trained and evaluated against human-drawn masks, yet remote-sensing annotations are often coarse, incomplete, or misaligned; high overlap scores may then reflect agreement with imperfect labels rather than faithfulness to the image, creating an evaluation paradox. We introduce Contrastive Mask Fidelity (CMF), a training-free, reference-free metric that scores competing class masks directly against image evidence. CMF composites keep and erase counterfactual views of each mask and asks a frozen vision-language judge whether class evidence is concentrated inside the mask and absent outside. We validate CMF on controlled mask corruptions, then audit 10,731 image-class pairs across ten remote-sensing benchmarks using candidate masks from Seg-Probe, a training-free open-vocabulary probe built on SegEarth-OV3 that outperforms prior baselines on nine of ten datasets. The audit reveals systematic, class-dependent annotation distortion: man-made classes such as buildings, roads, and cars favor the candidate mask on 62-85% of pairs, whereas ambiguous land cover more often favors human annotations. On a blinded three-annotator consensus, CMF matches expert judgment on 81% of pairs, exceeding keep-only scoring, model confidence, and a trained label-quality baseline. Finally, conservative class-wise arbitration yields supervision that improves cross-domain transfer over raw annotations and matched replacement controls, positioning CMF as a scalable tool for auditing ground truth rather than presuming it infallible.

</details>


### [170] [View-Adaptive Renderer for View-Consistent 2D-to-3D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09110)
*U-Chae Jun,Jaeeun Ko,Jiwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种视角自适应的神经渲染框架，通过视图自适应渲染器和自注意力融合模块，在存在部分不一致多视图输入的情况下实现鲁棒的单图像3D重建，无需扩散SDS监督即可达到接近SOTA的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单目3D生成流程（先多视图合成再用NeRF重建）存在固有投影歧义，导致生成视点间视觉不连续，进而造成3D重建不准确。现有解决方案要么计算负担过重，要么无法充分解决合成视图间的不一致问题。

Method: 提出视角自适应神经渲染框架：1) 视图自适应神经渲染器可独立纠正依赖于视角的误差，同时共享全局特征骨干以保持结构一致性；2) 自注意力融合模块自适应整合多视角信息，确保几何一致性；3) 主要依赖光度渲染损失加轻量注意力正则化，无需扩散SDS监督。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法持续提升3D重建保真度，在不使用扩散SDS监督的情况下达到接近最先进的性能，实现了准确性与效率的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该框架通过视图自适应校正与自注意力融合的协同设计，有效解决了多视图不一致条件下的3D重建难题，兼具高精度与计算效率，具有较强的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D shapes from a single image remains a fundamental yet challenging problem in computer vision. Traditional monocular 3D generation pipelines typically synthesize multiple views from a single input image before applying Neural Radiance Field (NeRF)-based reconstruction. However, inherent projective ambiguities often produce visual discontinuities across generated viewpoints, leading to inaccuracies in reconstructed 3D models. Current solutions either incur significant additional computational burdens or fail to adequately resolve practical inconsistencies between synthesized views. To address these limitations, we propose a novel viewpoint-adaptive neural rendering framework that enables robust 3D reconstruction even when given partially inconsistent multi-view inputs. Our approach introduces view-adaptive neural renderers that independently correct viewpoint-dependent errors while simultaneously sharing a global feature backbone to preserve structural coherence. Furthermore, we propose a self-attention fusion module that adaptively integrates multi-view information, ensuring geometric consistency without relying heavily on indirect regularizations or computationally intensive methods. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our method consistently improves 3D reconstruction fidelity. Importantly, our approach achieves near state-of-the-art performance without diffusion-based SDS supervision, relying primarily on photometric rendering loss with lightweight attention regularizers. This balance between accuracy and efficiency makes the proposed framework highly practical for real-world applications.

</details>


### [171] [Visual Distortion Detection in UGC Images Using Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09122)
*Ziheng Jia,Yingji Liang,Jiaying Qian,Xiongkuo Min*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VIGIL，一种基于大语言模型（LMM）架构的视觉失真检测方法。通过构建140K规模的高质量训练集（VIGIL-140K），并利用LLM解码器的多层特征作为多个同步检测器，同时保留非失真类预测中的失真线索来缓解合成到真实（S2A）场景下的前后景分离模糊问题，在域内合成失真检测和S2A任务上均优于强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大多模态模型（LMM）的图像质量评估方法主要依赖文本驱动的监督微调（SFT），在检测精度上存在明显不足；同时，以合成失真图像为主要训练数据的方法在真实场景中泛化能力差，存在合成到真实（S2A）的显著泛化鸿沟。这两大问题构成了本文的核心研究动机。

Method: 本文提出VIGIL框架：1）从超1000K样本中通过严格质量过滤和精心设计的失真注入构建VIGIL-140K训练集，覆盖8类主要合成失真；2）利用LLM解码器的不同层作为多个检测器，使用多层级特征进行同步失真检测；3）保留非失真类预测中的失真线索，以缓解S2A问题中常见的前后景（FG-BG）分离模糊。

Result: 经过程后处理，VIGIL在域内合成失真检测和S2A任务上均一致性地优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: VIGIL通过大规模高质量训练数据构建与多层级LLM特征协同检测机制，有效提升了失真检测的精度，并在从合成失真迁移到真实失真的S2A场景中展现了更强的泛化能力，为该方向提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: The localized depiction of perceptual quality has long been a crucial, yet underexplored, challenge in image quality assessment (IQA). Existing approaches based on large multimodal models (LMMs) predominantly rely on text-driven supervised fine-tuning (SFT).
  However, this training paradigm exhibits notable limitations in detection accuracy. Moreover, synthetically distorted images, which are often used as the primary training data source,
  show a significant generalization gap when deployed in real-world scenarios; thus, the \textbf{synthetic-to-authentic (\textit{S2A})} problem represents a critical challenge. Motivated by these issues, we propose \textbf{\textit{VIGIL}}, which leverages the LMM architecture for precise visual distortion detection. From a candidate pool of over 1000K samples, we construct the \textbf{\textit{VIGIL-140K}} training set, which consists of over 140K distorted images. These images are obtained through rigorous quality filtering and carefully crafted distortion injection, covering 8 major synthetic distortion categories.
  Our model leverages different layers of the large language model (LLM) decoder, treating them as \textit{multiple detectors} that perform synchronous distortion detection using multi-level features. Additionally, we retain distortion cues from predictions assigned to the non-distortion class, which helps mitigate the ambiguous foreground-background (\textit{FG-BG}) separation commonly encountered in the \textit{S2A} problem.
  After post-processing, our model consistently outperforms strong baselines on both in-domain synthetic distortion detection and \textit{S2A} tasks.

</details>


### [172] [When Latents Forget Pixels: Restoring Fidelity in Diffusion Transformer Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09133)
*Yu Shi,Yuyao Zhang,Yu-wing Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PGSR框架，通过在VAE压缩前提取LR像素证据并在条件端和解码器端两个阶段重用它，克服扩散Transformer在潜在空间中因VAE压缩瓶颈导致的幻觉细节问题，改善生成式超分辨率的真实感与保真度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大生成模型在图像超分辨率上取得了出色的感知质量，但在保真度上存在不足。作者观察到基于潜在表示的扩散Transformer（DiT）存在一个关键局限：VAE的压缩瓶颈削弱了细粒度空间信息，导致生成细节产生与输入图像弱相关的幻觉。

Method: 提出像素接地超分辨率（PGSR）框架：从升采样的LR图像中提取VAE压缩前的像素证据，并在两个阶段复用。一是条件端轨迹引导，将LR像素证据与潜在LR条件融合以引导潜在恢复轨迹；二是解码器端像素接地，将多尺度像素特征注入冻结的VAE解码器以接地最终渲染。保持潜在自编码器和主流匹配骨干冻结，仅训练轻量恢复模块，并研究了高效的局部窗口注意力变体以提升高分辨率效率。

Result: 大量实验表明，PGSR改善了真实感-保真度权衡，相比现有潜在生成式SR方法，生成了更忠实、视觉上更令人信服的结果。

Conclusion: 从表示学习的视角重新审视生成式SR，验证了在潜在生成框架中显式保留和复用LR像素证据对改善保真度的有效性，为基于大模型潜在式超分辨率的忠实性提升提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Image super-resolution (SR) with large generative models has recently achieved remarkable perceptual quality, yet maintaining fidelity to the LR observation remains challenging. In particular, we observe that diffusion transformers (DiTs) built on latent representations suffer from a critical limitation: the compression bottleneck of the VAE weakens fine-grained spatial information, leading to hallucinated details that are weakly grounded in the input image. In this work, we revisit generative SR from a representation perspective and propose a pixel-grounded super-resolution (PGSR) framework that preserves LR-observed pixel evidence before VAE compression and reuses it throughout restoration. Instead of relying solely on the compressed latent condition, PGSR extracts pre-VAE pixel evidence from the upsampled LR image and reuses it at two stages. First, Condition-Side Trajectory Guidance fuses LR-derived pixel evidence with the latent LR condition to guide the latent restoration trajectory. Second, Decoder-Side Pixel Grounding injects multi-scale pixel features into the frozen VAE decoder to ground the final rendering with LR-observed cues. To efficiently adapt large pretrained DiT models, we keep the latent autoencoder and main flow-matching backbone frozen, and train only lightweight restoration modules. We further study an efficient local-window attention variant for improved high-resolution efficiency and scalability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PGSR improves the realism--fidelity trade-off and produces more faithful, visually convincing results than existing latent generative SR approaches.

</details>


### [173] [Bright-Channel Retinex Enhancement with a Conditional Overdispered-Noise Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09137)
*Jongpil Jeong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的微光图像增强方法，结合局部亮通道照度估计、Retinex除法与边缘保持去噪；利用条件负二项伪计数模型刻画除法放大的异方差噪声，反射率采用逐像素极大似然估计。在LOL-v1数据集上达到17.74dB/0.739，处理速度约43FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微光图像增强中，Retinex除法会放大噪声，现有方法缺乏对异方差噪声的系统性建模；同时训练型方法需要大量数据且泛化性有限，而传统方法精度不足，需要在效率和准确性之间取得平衡。

Method: 1) 通过局部亮通道估计照度；2) 在固定照度估计条件下，用条件负二项（NB）伪计数模型刻画除法放大的异方差噪声；3) 反射率采用逐像素极大似然估计（对零值观测给出边界解）；4) 应用照度滤波与范围裁剪；5) 最终使用固定带宽双边滤波进行边缘保持去噪（作为经验近似而非精确贝叶斯解）。

Result: 在LOL-v1数据集上获得平均PSNR/SSIM为17.74dB/0.739，为所评估的传统方法中的最高值；400x600图像在Apple M2 Pro CPU上以约43FPS处理。

Conclusion: 该方法为训练-free微光增强提供了一种以噪声建模驱动的解决方案，NB模型作为诊断工具而非校准传感器模型，以较低计算成本实现优于现有传统方法的增强性能。

Abstract: I present a training-free low-light enhancement method that combines local bright-channel illumination estimation, Retinex division, and edge-preserving denoising. For a fixed illumination estimate, a conditional Negative -Binominal psueduo-count method characterises the heteroscedastic noise amplified by division. The unconstrained reflectance ratio is the pixelwise maximum-likelihood estimate, with a boundary solution for zero-valued observations; the implemented estimate additionally applies illumination filtering and range clipping. The NB model is a diagnostic noise analysis rather than a calibrated sensor model, and the final fixed-bandwidth bilateral filter is an empirical approximation rather than the exact Bayesian solution. On the LOL-v1 dataset, the methodobtains mean PSNR/SSIM of 17.74dB/0.739, the highest values among the evaluated with conventional methods. A 400X600 image is processed at approximately 43 FPS on an Apple M2 Pro CPU.

</details>


### [174] [CodecArena: Codec Quality Assessment via Visual Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09139)
*Jiaye Fu,Weiqi Li,Qiankun Gao,Yanchen Zhao,Xiandong Meng,Jian Zhang,Siwei Ma,Jiaqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了CodecArena，首个用于视频编码质量评估的视觉-语言框架，将编码器评估建模为源条件化的对比推理，并采用Facet-GRPO视觉强化学习方案，通过五个保真度维度（身份、物体、文本、纹理、时间一致性）对齐成对编码器偏好。配套构建了全自动偏好数据集CodecArena-1K和人工排序基准CodecArena-Bench，实验证明其在跨源内容上超越现有感知指标和视觉语言评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低码率和超低码率视频编码领域不断进步，端到端和生成式编码器都能生成视觉上令人满意的重建，但现有主导指标LPIPS和DISTS衡量的是特征和纹理相似度而非内容保真度：重建即使产生错误人脸或把文本模糊成逼真笔画也能获得高分，而人类会立刻拒绝。现有指标与人类感知存在根本性偏差。

Method: 提出CodecArena框架：1) 将编码器评估形式化为源条件化的对比推理任务（参考视频与其重建之间的比较）；2) 设计Facet-GRPO视觉强化学习方案，通过五个保真度方面（身份、物体、文本、纹理、时间一致性）对齐成对编码器偏好；3) 方面锚定奖励利用自动推导的方面方向作为弱锚点（而非人工逐方面标注），防止单一子分数主导整体偏好；4) 构建CodecArena-1K全自动偏好数据集（1,500个比较组，覆盖传统、神经和生成式编码器，融合视觉-语言和客观监督）以及CodecArena-Bench人工排序基准（源不相交视频用于公平外域评估）。

Result: 在源不相交内容上、跨越多种编码器和码率条件下，CodecArena与人类判断的一致性达到最先进水平，超越了感知指标（如LPIPS、DISTS）和先前的视觉语言评估器。

Conclusion: CodecArena验证了视觉-语言框架结合强化学习和多方面保真度对齐能够有效弥合现有质量指标与人类内容保真度感知之间的鸿沟，为低码率视频编码质量评估提供了更可靠、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Video coding is advancing into the low and ultra-low bitrate regime, driven by end-to-end codecs that replace the hand-crafted pipeline with jointly optimized neural networks and generative codecs that exploit the priors of video generation models. Yet the dominant metrics, LPIPS and DISTS, measure feature and texture similarity rather than content fidelity: a reconstruction that hallucinates a wrong face or blurs text into convincing strokes can still score well, even when a human rejects it instantly. To address this, we propose CodecArena, the first vision-language framework for video coding quality assessment, casting codec evaluation as source-conditioned comparative reasoning between a reference and its reconstructions. We optimize CodecArena with Facet-GRPO, a visual reinforcement learning scheme that aligns pairwise codec preferences while grounding the verdict in five fidelity facets: identity, objects, text, texture, and temporal consistency. Its facet-anchored reward uses automatically derived facet directions as weak anchors, rather than human per-facet labels, to prevent any single sub-score from dominating the holistic preference and to yield interpretable fine-grained quality judgments. To support training and evaluation in this underexplored regime, we construct two complementary resources: CodecArena-1K, a fully automatic preference dataset of 1,500 comparison groups built from traditional, neural, and generative codec reconstructions with fused vision-language and objective supervision; and CodecArena-Bench, a human-ranked benchmark with source-disjoint videos for fair out-of-domain evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CodecArena achieves state-of-the-art agreement with human judgments on source-disjoint content across diverse codecs and bitrates, surpassing perceptual metrics and prior vision-language evaluators.

</details>


### [175] [UniMoFlow: Grounding Instruction-Driven 3D Human Motion Editing in Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09143)
*Yilei Hua,Beibei Jing,Ce Zheng,Hanyu Zhou,Yawei Luo,Wei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种指令驱动的3D人体运动编辑统一框架，通过数据、架构和推理三层面的协同设计，解决现有方法在时空定位、语义接地和内容保留方面的不足。数据层面基于闭环合成-验证流程构建大规模数据集Omni-MoEdit；架构层面提出统一潜流匹配模型UniMoFlow；推理层面引入SAFE源锚定流编辑机制，并补充语义感知评估指标，全面提升了运动编辑的各项性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有指令驱动的3D人体运动编辑方法存在两大瓶颈：一是训练自由的生成模型适配方式控制效果欠佳；二是依赖人工标注的三元组训练数据规模有限、语义多样性不足，难以支撑精确的时空定位与丰富的语义接地需求。

Method: 在三个层面协同设计：(1) 数据层面：开发闭环合成-验证流水线，生成覆盖身体部位、幅度、时间、动作和风格等多维度编辑的大规模数据集Omni-MoEdit；(2) 架构层面：提出UniMoFlow统一潜流匹配模型，在生成与编辑任务间共享语义和运动学知识；(3) 推理层面：提出SAFE（源锚定流编辑）机制，实现可控的源锚定细化；同时引入语义感知评估指标，以正确处理与单一真实参考固有偏差的有效编辑。

Result: 大量实验表明，该方法在目标文本对齐、编辑有效性和循环一致性方面取得显著提升，同时保持了有竞争力的源保真度和文生运动生成质量。

Conclusion: 将运动编辑直接嵌入文生运动生成框架，通过数据-架构-推理三层次的统一设计，有效突破了训练数据瓶颈和控制质量问题，为3D人体运动编辑提供了可扩展且有理论支撑的解决方案。

Abstract: Instruction-driven editing of 3D human motion requires precise spatiotemporal localization, rich semantic grounding, and strict preservation of unmodified content. Existing methods either resort to training-free adaptation of generative models or rely solely on triplet supervision; however, adaptation often yields suboptimal control, and manually curated triplet datasets remain severely limited in scale and semantic diversity. To overcome this bottleneck, we ground motion editing directly within text-to-motion generation across data, architecture, and inference. At the data level, we develop a closed-loop synthesis-and-verification pipeline that produces Omni-MoEdit, a large-scale dataset spanning body-part, amplitude, temporal, action, and style edits. At the architectural level, we introduce UniMoFlow, a unified latent flow-matching model that shares broad semantic and kinematic knowledge between generation and editing. At the inference level, SAFE (Source-Anchored Flow Editing) complements UniMoFlow with controllable, source-anchored refinement. Furthermore, we augment standard evaluations with semantics-aware metrics to account for valid edits that inherently deviate from a single ground-truth reference. Extensive experiments demonstrate improved target-text alignment, edit effectiveness, and cycle consistency, while maintaining competitive source fidelity and text-to-motion generation quality.

</details>


### [176] [Right Answer, Wrong Heat: Explanation-Aware Evaluation and Thermal-Grounded Feedback for MLLMs on Infrared Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09145)
*Yongsong Huang,Xiaofeng Liu,Tomo Miyazaki,Yaohou Fan,Shinichiro Omachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向红外图像的、解释感知的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）评估框架，将答案正确性、输出级解释根基性（groundedness）与热红外根基性分开度量。通过双LLM共识裁判和人工锚定校准，发现正确答案可能依赖弱证据或可见光证据；移除红外图像（仅提供可见光渲染图）会削弱热根基性而准确率几乎不变，且此现象在更强模型中更显著。进一步提出无需训练的反馈机制（TGF），可在不改变答案的前提下改进解释的热根基性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLM在红外图像任务中仅以答案准确率评估，但正确答案不保证模型解释真正基于红外热证据。需要一种能区分"答对了"与"真的依据红外证据解释"的评估与改进机制，以保障红外场景理解的可靠性。

Method: 提出解释感知评估框架，将答案正确性、输出层解释根基性和热根基性分别度量；使用双大语言模型共识裁判（Dual-LLM Consensus Judge），并辅以人工锚定校准检查；提出无需训练的Thermal-Grounded Feedback（TGF）反馈循环，诊断解释侧失败并在保持答案不变的前提下修订解释。

Result: 研究发现：正确答案仍可能基于弱证据或可见光证据；移除红外图像（仅保留可见光渲染）会显著削弱热根基性但准确率几乎不变，且此现象在更强模型中更明显，但当红外图像仍可用时该现象消失；在局部配对输入验证中，TGF在不改变答案的前提下提升了解释侧的热根基性。

Conclusion: 未来用于红外场景理解的MLLM应被评估和开发为产生热根基性的解释，而非仅仅给出准确的答案，从而保障可信赖的红外推理能力。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly applied to infrared images, where they are commonly scored by answer accuracy alone. However, a correct answer does not ensure that the model's explanation is grounded in infrared thermal evidence. We introduce an explanation-aware evaluation framework that separates answer correctness, output-level explanation groundedness, and thermal grounding for infrared visual questions. Using a Dual-LLM Consensus Judge with a preliminary human-anchor calibration check, we find that correct answers can still rely on weak or visible-light evidence; withholding the original infrared image and showing only a visible-like rendering erodes thermal grounding with little accuracy change; and this erosion is observed most strongly for more capable models but disappears when infrared remains available. We further propose Thermal-Grounded Feedback (TGF), a training-free feedback loop that diagnoses explanation-side failures and revises the explanation while preserving the selected answer. On local paired-input validation, TGF improves explanation-side grounding without changing answers. These findings suggest that future trustworthy MLLMs for infrared scene understanding should be evaluated and developed to produce thermally grounded explanations rather than merely accurate answers.

</details>


### [177] [Multi-Submap Implicit Neural SLAM with Local-to-Global Loop Closure for Large-Scale Scene Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09146)
*Tianchen Deng,Chongdi Wang,Nailin Wang,Lei Zhao,Ziqi Ma,Tianjun Zhang,Zhe Liu,Danwei Wang,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于多子地图架构和双层回环检测的大规模神经SLAM系统（MSN-SLAM），通过渐进式建图策略、光流跟踪模块、局部到全局的回环闭合框架以及子地图间在线蒸馏算法，解决了NeRF-SLAM在大规模场景中的灾难性遗忘和轨迹漂移问题，在重建质量和定位鲁棒性上优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NeRF-based SLAM在小规模场景重建中表现优异，但在扩展到大规模复杂环境时面临两大核心挑战：灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting）和累积轨迹漂移（accumulated trajectory drift），亟需一种可扩展的解决方案来满足真实世界机器人感知和数字孪生需求。

Method: 提出多子地图架构与双层回环闭合机制：(1) 渐进式建图策略动态分配神经子地图，保证高保真重建同时避免内存爆炸；(2) 基于光流的跟踪模块处理剧烈运动下的鲁棒位姿估计；(3) 利用基础模型（foundation model）进行全局描述子提取的局部到全局回环闭合框架，提升不同视点下的重定位精度；(4) 在后端优化过程中设计子地图间在线蒸馏算法，强制重叠子地图边界处的几何与外观一致性。此外还开发了定制手持机电平台用于数据采集与验证。

Result: 在公开基准数据集和自建的大规模室内外数据集上进行广泛评估，并在板载计算单元上直接部署验证。实验结果表明，该方法在重建质量和定位鲁棒性方面均优于当前最先进的神经SLAM方法，展现出良好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: MSN-SLAM为大规模神经SLAM提供了可扩展的解决方案，通过多子地图管理与双层回环闭合有效缓解了灾难性遗忘和轨迹漂移问题，适用于真实机器人感知与数字孪生应用，代码将开源发布。

Abstract: Neural Radiance Fields (NeRF)-based SLAM has demonstrated impressive results in small-scale scene reconstruction, yet scaling these methods to extensive, complex environments remains challenging due to catastrophic forgetting and accumulated trajectory drift. This paper presents a robust, large-scale neural SLAM system featuring a multi-submap architecture and a dual-tier loop closure mechanism. Specifically, we propose a progressive mapping strategy that dynamically allocates neural submaps to maintain high-fidelity representations without memory explosion. For robust pose estimation, an optical-flow-based tracking module is integrated to handle aggressive motions. To address global consistency, we introduce a local-to-global loop closure framework leveraging the foundation model for high-performance global descriptor extraction, significantly enhancing relocalization accuracy under varying viewpoints. Furthermore, an inter-submap online distillation algorithm is designed during back-end optimization to enforce geometric and appearance consistency across overlapping submap boundaries. To validate the system, we developed a customized handheld mechatronic platform and conducted extensive evaluations on both public benchmarks and our large-scale indoor-outdoor datasets. Experimental results, including direct deployment on an onboard computing unit, demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art neural SLAM methods in reconstruction quality and localization robustness, providing a scalable solution for real-world robotic perception and digital twinning. We will release the code publicly on \href{https://github.com/dtc111111/MSN-SLAM}{https://github.com/dtc111111/MSN-SLAM} .

</details>


### [178] [RefineAny3D: Depth Refinement as Semantic Alignment for Monocular 3D Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09147)
*Zhihao Zhang,Gengwei Zhang,Tianlong Chen,Xiaoming Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefineAny3D是一个视觉语言模型，将单目3D检测中的物体级深度细化转化为视觉对齐问题而非数值回归问题，通过扩展VLM词表加入动作token并使用链式思维数据集监督，作为后处理步骤可一致提升闭集检测器、开放词汇检测器和3D自动标注工具的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度基础模型虽然具备强大的零样本泛化能力，但缺乏3D检测所需的物体级精度——将一个SOTA深度基础模型直接替换强检测器的预测深度反而会导致精度下降，甚至低于检测器自身的预测。作者不试图端到端地提升检测器或深度模型的精度，而是将物体级深度细化视为独立任务。

Method: 核心洞察：深度误差在图像空间有直接视觉特征——投影到图像上时，正确放置的3D框紧密包围物体，过远的框投影偏小，过近的框投影偏大。因此深度细化被转化为视觉对齐问题而非数值回归问题。具体实现：（1）扩展VLM词表加入动作token，用分类决策替代数值深度输出；（2）在大规模链式思维数据集上监督训练，每个决策都基于明确的视觉证据；（3）作为单次后处理步骤应用于任意检测器。

Result: RefineAny3D作为单一后处理步骤，在闭集检测器、开放词汇检测器和3D自动标注工具上均取得一致提升，且无需重新训练即可泛化到新类别、新场景和新相机。

Conclusion: 物体级深度细化不应被视为数值回归问题，而应被视为视觉对齐问题。基于VLM的深度修正方法能有效捕捉深度误差的图像空间视觉特征，从而在无需训练的前提下广泛适用于各类3D检测框架。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object detection spans two regimes: closed-set detectors operating within a fixed category vocabulary, and open-vocabulary detectors that localize arbitrary categories by leveraging depth foundation models for 3D geometry. We find that current depth foundation models, despite their strong zero-shot generalization, lack the object-level precision 3D detection demands: substituting a state-of-the-art depth foundation model for a strong detector's predicted depth degrades accuracy, even falling below the detector's own prediction. Rather than pushing detectors or depth models to be more accurate end-to-end, we treat object-level depth refinement as a stand-alone task and present RefineAny3D, a vision-language model that corrects depth without ever predicting a numerical value. Our key insight is that depth error has a direct visual signature in image space: when projected onto the image, a correctly placed box tightly encloses the object, while a too-far box projects too small and a too-close box projects too large. Depth refinement thus reduces to a visual alignment problem rather than a metric regression problem, which we instantiate by extending the VLM's vocabulary with action tokens that replace numerical depth output with categorical decisions, and by supervising the model on a large-scale chain-of-thought dataset that grounds each decision in explicit visual evidence. Applied as a single post-hoc step, RefineAny3D delivers consistent gains across closed-set detectors, open-vocabulary detectors, and 3D auto-labeling tools, and generalizes to novel categories, scenes, and cameras without retraining.

</details>


### [179] [OGG-FR: Orthogonal Gradient Gaming and Frequency Rectification for Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Infrared Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09150)
*Yongsong Huang,Qingzhong Wang,Xiaofeng Liu,Tomo Miyazaki,Yaohou Fan,Shinichiro Omachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OGG-FR，一个即插即用的优化框架，通过将频率梯度正交分解为冗余平行分量和正交创新分量来缓解UAV红外图像超分任务中像素域与频率域损失梯度冲突的问题，并在UAV热成像基准上取得了广泛效果提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UAV红外图像超分需要在资源受限平台上部署轻量模型，但多损失训练不稳定。现有像素域+频率域损失组合在低对比度、有限高频内容和传感器噪声下，其梯度往往弱对齐或相互冲突，导致优化歧义。

Method: OGG-FR将频率梯度分解为相对于像素梯度的冗余平行分量和正交创新分量。在冲突区间，使用MGDA计算安全基础梯度并添加方差校正的正交创新；在兼容区间，丢弃冗余平行信息，依据高频残差估计的置信度分数注入正交创新。

Result: 在UAV热成像基准上，BI和BD退化下×4和×8尺度均取得广泛增益；梯度分析验证了所提出的冲突感知更新规则的有效性。

Conclusion: OGG-FR作为一种即插即用的优化框架，通过正交梯度博弈和频率校正有效解决了像素域与频率域损失的梯度冲突问题，为资源受限的UAV红外超分任务提供了稳定且有效的训练方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) infrared image super-resolution aims to recover weak thermal structures for deployment on resource-constrained platforms; lightweight models are therefore preferred, but multi-loss training can be unstable. A common strategy combines pixel-domain and frequency-domain objectives; however, low contrast, limited high-frequency content, and sensor-specific noise often make their gradients weakly aligned or conflicting. To address this optimization ambiguity, we propose Orthogonal Gradient Gaming and Frequency Rectification (OGG-FR), a plug-and-play optimization framework that decomposes the frequency gradient into a redundant parallel component and an orthogonal innovation component relative to the pixel gradient. In the conflict regime, OGG-FR computes a safe base gradient using the Multiple Gradient Descent Algorithm (MGDA) and adds a variance-rectified orthogonal innovation; in the compatible regime, it discards redundant parallel information and injects the orthogonal innovation according to a confidence score estimated from the high-frequency residual. Experimental results on the UAV thermal benchmark show broad gains under BI and BD degradations at $\times 4$ and $\times 8$ scales, while gradient analyses support the effectiveness of the proposed conflict-aware update rule.

</details>


### [180] [LightAIR: Lightweight Action Inversion and Riemannian Rectification for Text-based Person Anomaly Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09152)
*Yulun Zhang,Zixu Li,Zhiwei Chen,Zhiheng Fu,Wenbo Wang,Zihang Qiu,Zhilin Wang,Ruxin Wang,Yupeng Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了LightAIR网络，通过轻量级动作反演算子提取纯动作特征、正交零空间投影实现严格前向解耦、以及黎曼梯度修正模块约束反向传播轨迹，解决文本行人异常搜索中外观与动作特征耦合和捷径学习问题，在TPAS和TIPR数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本行人搜索仅匹配静态外观属性，忽略了动态动作信息。TPAS任务需要模型同时定位微观层级的异常行为并匹配宏观层级的行人外观，但现有方法存在三方面限制：外部显式姿态估计器在非约束监控场景下脆弱不可靠；隐式学习在像素级纠缠下出现视觉解耦失败，外观信息易淹没并污染细微动作特征；在欧氏空间中对困难负样本（"同外观不同动作"）做对比优化会引发严重的捷径学习。

Method: LightAIR包含三大核心模块：(1) 轻量级动作反演算子，以文本语义先验作为锚点提取纯动作特征，克服视觉固有耦合；(2) 正交零空间投影，将外观特征约束到动作特征的正交补空间中，保证严格的前向解耦；(3) 梯度修正模块，计算黎曼梯度以约束反向传播轨迹，迫使梯度流严格沿着保持解耦性质的切空间更新，切断有害的捷径。

Result: 在广泛使用的TPAS和TIPR数据集上进行的大量实验表明，LightAIR显著优于现有的最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过动作反演、正交零空间投影和黎曼梯度修正三个策略的组合，LightAIR有效解决了TPAS任务中外观与动作特征解耦失败以及困难负样本导致的捷径学习问题，在监控场景下的行人异常搜索任务上取得了领先性能。

Abstract: Traditional Text-based Person Search (TPS) is typically limited to matching static appearance attributes, severely neglecting dynamic action information. The Text-based Person Anomaly Search (TPAS) task bridges this gap, requiring models to locate micro-level specific abnormal behaviors while matching macro-level appearance of pedestrians. However, current TPAS methods face fundamental limitations: external explicit pose estimators are fragile in unconstrained surveillance scenarios, and implicit learning encounters visual decoupling failure under pixel-level entanglement, causing dominant appearance information to easily swallow and contaminate subtle action features. Furthermore, performing contrastive optimization on hard negative samples (``same appearance, different actions'') in conventional Euclidean spaces induces severe shortcut learning. To address these, we propose the Lightweight Action Inversion and Riemannian rectification network (LightAIR). First, it introduces textual semantic priors as anchors via a lightweight action inversion operator to extract pure action features, thereby overcoming visual-inherent coupling. Subsequently, it employs orthogonal null-space projection to constrain appearance features within the orthogonal complement space of action features, guaranteeing strict forward decoupling. Finally, we designed a gradient rectification module that computes the Riemannian gradient to constrain the backpropagation trajectory, forcing the gradient flow to update strictly along the tangent space that preserves decoupling properties, thereby cutting off harmful shortcuts. Extensive experiments on the widely used TPAS and TIPR datasets demonstrate that LightAIR significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods. Codes are available at https://github.com/rainy-london/LightAIR

</details>


### [181] [Not All Visual Tokens Are Equally Safe to Remove:Consequence-Sensitive Visual Token Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09176)
*Jingbo Wen,Liang He,Mingyu Cao,Haoyu Wang,Minxuan Hu,Kangning Cui,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"后果敏感的视觉token压缩"方法，根据下游任务的潜在错误成本来分配视觉计算预算，而非传统方法那样假设所有错误代价相同。通过校准-分配两阶段流程，在相同总token预算下将高后果错误率从0.300降至0.133，并在混合工作负载中将代价加权错误降低38%，同时延迟比全分辨率推理低约21%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token压缩方法（基于注意力、冗余度、不确定性等准则）都隐式假设所有预测错误具有相同成本，但现实中不同错误的后果高度不对称——例如误读发票金额远比误分类背景颜色代价高昂。这种不对称性在现有方法中完全被忽视，导致计算资源未按潜在错误成本进行最优分配。

Method: 采用"先校准再分配"(calibrate-then-allocate)两阶段流程：离线阶段估计不同后果等级对应的错误-预算曲线（error-budget curves）；在线阶段利用问题或任务信息中可获得的后果信号，根据校准后的token预算动态分配计算资源。该方法通过token删除和分辨率重分配两种机制实现预算落地。

Result: 在受控的文档内任务基准上，高后果错误率从0.300降至0.133（相同总token预算），而基于内容驱动的分配器不优于均匀分配。该方法在三个密集视觉语言基准、两种预算实现机制、两种VLM架构和多种token选择策略上均表现一致。在现实混合工作负载中，代价加权错误降低38%，延迟比全分辨率推理低约21%。

Conclusion: 当错误成本不对称时，将token预算向高后果请求倾斜是显著有益的；当错误成本相等时均匀分配最优。论文揭示了分配边界（allocation frontier）：成本差距越大，跨请求的token转移收益越高。后果敏感分配原则在不同基准、架构和预算机制下具有很强的泛化能力。

Abstract: Visual token compression for vision--language models (VLMs) has largely relied on criteria such as attention, redundancy, and uncertainty to maximize average accuracy under a fixed compute budget, implicitly assuming that all errors carry equal cost. However, the consequence of an incorrect prediction on downstream tasks is rarely symmetric: misreading an invoice amount can be far more costly than misclassifying a background color. Motivated by this, we introduce consequence-sensitive visual token compression, which allocates visual computation across requests according to their potential error costs. Our method follows a calibrate-then-allocate procedure, estimating consequence-specific error-budget curves offline and applying the calibrated token budgets online using consequence signals available from question or task information. On a controlled within-task benchmark, high- and low-consequence questions are drawn from the same document images, so content alone cannot reveal which questions are costly to get wrong. In this setting, our method reduces high-stakes errors from 0.300 to 0.133 under the same total token budget, whereas a content-driven allocator performs no better than uniform allocation. Measuring how error rates change with token budget across different cost ratios, we derive an allocation frontier: uniform allocation is optimal when errors are equally costly, and token transfer toward high-consequence questions becomes increasingly beneficial as the cost gap grows. This allocation principle generalizes well across three dense vision-language benchmarks, two budget realization mechanisms (token deletion and resolution reallocation), two VLM architectures, and multiple token selection strategies. On a realistic mixed workload, consequence-sensitive allocation reduces cost-weighted error by 38% while achieving approximately 21% lower latency than full-resolution inference.

</details>


### [182] [Rethinking Medical Landmark Localization with Prototype Learning-based Progressive Offset Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09182)
*Jingxian Xu,Yuhao Huang,Rusi Chen,Yanfeng Zhou,Dong Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PPOC-LL，一种基于原型学习的渐进式偏移校正的参数经济型模型，用于医学图像中的精确地标定位。该模型通过多尺度动态感知、相似性驱动原型学习和误差感知可靠性正则化，在X光和超声的多个数据集上实现了准确度与模型复杂度之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像中精确的地标定位是临床定量测量和下游分析的基础步骤。虽然多阶段精细化的定位策略能缓解单阶段全局预测中固有的解剖学模糊性问题，但其高计算成本限制了实际临床应用。因此需要一种参数经济且高效的定位方法。

Method: PPOC-LL包含三个关键贡献：(1) 多尺度动态感知策略，用于patch级特征金字塔建模，驱动粗到细的地标优化；(2) 相似性驱动的原型学习机制，捕获信息丰富的局部语义以处理解剖学相似模式，实现稳健的偏移预测；(3) 基于容差平衡的误差感知可靠性正则化，用于稳定模型学习并提升整体性能。

Result: 在涵盖X光和超声模态的三个数据集（两个公开、一个私有）上进行了大规模验证，覆盖头颅测量、联合胎头及胎儿心脏地标。大量实验表明PPOC-LL在准确性和模型复杂度之间取得了令人满意的权衡，达到了良好的定位性能。

Conclusion: PPOC-LL通过参数经济的渐进式偏移校正策略，有效解决了医学图像地标定位中准确性、复杂度与解剖学模糊性之间的平衡问题，为临床实际应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate landmark localization in medical images is a fundamental step for quantitative clinical measurement and downstream analysis. Existing localization methods have advanced, among which multi-stage refinement is a superior solution. Although this strategy mitigates the anatomical ambiguity inherent in single-stage global predictions, its high computational cost limits practical applicability. In this work, we propose a parameter-economic model, PPOC-LL, which leverages Prototype learning-based Progressive Offset Correction for Landmark Localization. Our contribution is three-fold. First, to drive coarse-to-fine landmark optimization, we introduce a multi-scale dynamic perception strategy for patch-level feature pyramid modeling. Second, to effectively handle anatomically similar patterns, we design a similarity-driven prototype learning mechanism that captures informative local semantics for robust offset prediction. Last, to stabilize the model learning and improve the overall performance, we incorporate a novel error-aware reliability regularization via tolerance-based balancing. We collected a large validation cohort, including two public and one private datasets spanning X-ray and ultrasound modalities, covering cephalometric, symphysis-fetal head, and fetal heart landmarks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PPOC-LL achieves satisfactory performance with a favorable trade-off between accuracy and model complexity.

</details>


### [183] [RAGMesh with FaME-G2E: Long-Form Text-Driven 3D Face Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09186)
*Hao Li,Ju Dai,Feng Zhou,Mengting Shi,Haofei Wang,Zhen Song,Wei Zhou,Lei Li,Junjun Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGMesh，一个检索增强的细粒度文本驱动3D面部生成与编辑框架，并构建了大规模多模态数据集FaME-G2E（包含文本-网格标注和文本-blendshape配对样本），通过多尺度检索融合和自适应区域感知监督实现高保真面部合成与精确的区域编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本驱动3D面部生成方法主要对齐全局文本语义与面部结构，难以捕捉细粒度的局部形变（如眉部张力、脸颊收缩、不对称嘴部运动），导致几何保真度和编辑精度受限。作者旨在解决长文本描述到细粒度面部几何翻译这一挑战。

Method: 1) 构建FaME-G2E数据集，包含详细文本-网格标注和文本-blendshape配对样本；2) 提出RAGMesh检索增强框架，包含两个核心模块：Multi-Scale Retrieval Fusion (MSRF)模块在blendshape空间中检索并融合语义一致的全局和区域面部先验，抑制冲突的局部形变；Adaptive RAG-guided Supervision (AdaRAGS)区域感知约束显式对齐文本语义与对应面部区域。

Result: 在FaME-G2E上的大量实验表明，RAGMesh在局部几何精度、文本引导可控性、区域编辑精度和推理效率方面均优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RAGMesh通过检索增强框架有效解决了细粒度文本驱动3D面部建模中局部形变捕捉困难的问题，同时通过多尺度融合与区域感知监督提升了编辑的精确性和可控性，为统一3D面部生成与编辑提供了新范式。

Abstract: Text-driven 3D face generation and editing remains challenging due to the difficulty of translating long-form descriptions into fine-grained facial geometry. Existing methods primarily align global textual semantics with facial structures but often struggle to capture subtle local deformations, such as eyebrow tension, cheek contraction, and asymmetric mouth motions, resulting in limited geometric fidelity and editing precision. To facilitate fine-grained text-driven facial modeling, we first construct FaME-G2E, a large-scale multimodal dataset containing detailed text--mesh annotations and paired text--blendshape samples for unified 3D facial generation and editing. Based on this dataset, we propose RAGMesh, a retrieval-augmented framework that leverages text-correlated geometric priors to improve high-fidelity facial synthesis and editing. Specifically, the Multi-Scale Retrieval Fusion (MSRF) module retrieves semantically consistent global and regional facial priors and fuses them in the blendshape space, suppressing conflicting local deformations while preserving coherent deformation patterns. Furthermore, we introduce Adaptive RAG-guided Supervision (AdaRAGS), a region-aware constraint that explicitly aligns textual semantics with corresponding facial regions, enhancing regional controllability and editing accuracy. Extensive experiments on FaME-G2E demonstrate that RAGMesh achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art methods in local geometric accuracy, text-guided controllability, regional editing precision, and inference efficiency. Video demo is available at https://youtu.be/Yr0_XkpWcNk, and the source code and dataset will be released upon paper acceptance.

</details>


### [184] [NBA_Streaming: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Fine-Grained Basketball Commentary Generation in Continuous Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09200)
*Lifang Wu,Yuyang Wu,Yangdong Gao,Fengyu Liu,Ya Jing,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NBA_Streaming，一个大规模在线篮球比赛解说生成基准，包含307小时直播视频和约35K个时间对齐事件，并提出了一个因果两阶段框架（完成优先定位+球心语义接地）用于流式视频中的实时解说生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有篮球解说生成方法主要针对预分割片段或完整视频设计，不适用于连续流式输入；同时现有数据集在球员身份、细粒度动作、事件属性和连贯事件链方面标注不足，限制了生成解说的事实丰富度。

Method: （1）构建NBA_Streaming基准：307小时比赛视频，约35K个时间对齐事件，标注事件边界、球员身份、细粒度动作、事件链和自然语言解说；（2）提出因果两阶段框架：完成优先定位（completion-first localization）用于从观测流中识别完整事件，结合球心语义接地（ball-centric semantic grounding）组织场景、事件、身份和行为线索进行解说生成。

Result: 实验表明NBA_Streaming具有挑战性，现有基线在在线时序、事实接地和细粒度描述上表现不佳；所提出的框架持续优于强基线，但仍有差距，说明该基准对流式体育视频理解与生成具有重要价值。

Conclusion: NBA_Streaming填补了流式体育视频解说生成领域的空白，提供了从孤立片段到连续流的统一评估（事件定位、响应可靠性、事实接地、解说质量，均受因果约束），为未来研究提供了有价值的基准和基线框架。

Abstract: Live basketball commentary generation requires determining when an event is sufficiently observable and describing it before subsequent events unfold. However, existing methods are primarily designed for pre-segmented clips or complete videos, making them unsuitable for continuous streams. Existing datasets also provide limited supervision for player identities, fine-grained actions, event attributes, and coherent event chains, restricting the factual richness of generated commentary. To address these limitations, we introduce NBA_Streaming, a large-scale benchmark for online fine-grained basketball commentary generation. It contains 307 hours of basketball broadcasts and approximately 35K temporally aligned events, with annotations of event boundaries, player identities, fine-grained actions, event chains, and natural-language commentary. By moving from isolated clips to continuous streams, NBA_Streaming enables unified evaluation of event localization, response reliability, factual grounding, and commentary quality under causal constraints. We further propose a causal two-stage framework that combines completion-first localization with ball-centric semantic grounding, enabling the system to identify complete events from observed streams and organize scene, event, identity, and action cues for commentary generation. Extensive experiments reveal the difficulty of NBA_Streaming, where existing baselines struggle with online timing, factual grounding, and fine-grained description. Our framework consistently improves over strong alternatives, while the remaining gap highlights NBA_Streaming as a valuable benchmark for streaming sports video understanding and generation.

</details>


### [185] [PatchHead: Learning Spatial Patch Evidence for Generalizable AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09223)
*Shengbo Qi,Hongyi Fang,Benjia Zhou,Rui Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PatchHead，一种轻量级空间聚合头，通过保留DINO patch token的二维结构并聚合邻域证据来改善AI生成图像检测器的跨生成器/跨数据集泛化能力。在九个跨数据集基准上，PatchHead以仅增加8.6%可训练参数和0.08%额外FLOPs的代价，将平均平衡准确率从91.6%提升至94.6%，最差情况准确率从82.4%提升至89.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI生成图像检测器在训练与测试图像来自不同生成器或数据集时泛化能力较差。尽管DINO等视觉基础模型提供了丰富的空间表征，但现有检测器通常只使用全局聚合的CLS token进行分类，作者假设这种全局聚合方式掩盖了空间分布式的生成痕迹（generation traces），从而限制了跨域泛化能力。

Method: 提出PatchHead，一种轻量级空间聚合头，其核心设计是：(1) 保留DINO patch token的二维空间组织结构，而非压缩为单一CLS token；(2) 在相邻区域间整合证据以捕捉空间分布的伪造痕迹。训练时冻结预训练的DINO骨干网络，仅优化插入的LoRA适配器、PatchHead和辅助投影头。

Result: 在九个跨数据集基准（涵盖人工整理和真实世界场景）上，PatchHead在七个数据集上排名第一，在其余两个数据集上排名第二。相比最强基线方法，平均平衡准确率从91.6%提升到94.6%（+3.0点），最差情况准确率从82.4%提升到89.4%（+6.9点），且仅引入8.6%额外可训练参数和0.08%额外FLOPs。定性分析表明PatchHead降低了类条件域偏差，并将表征从内容主导的显著性转向空间分布的 authenticity 证据。

Conclusion: 空间patch聚合比基于单一CLS token的全局表征在跨生成器和数据集场景下具有更可靠的迁移能力，这为理解AI生成图像检测器的跨域泛化提供了表征层面的解释。PatchHead以极低的额外计算和参数量成本显著提升了检测器的鲁棒性。

Abstract: AI-generated image detectors generalize poorly when their training and test images originate from different generators or datasets. Despite the rich spatial representations produced by vision foundation models like DINO, existing detectors typically classify images using only the globally aggregated CLS token. We hypothesize that globally aggregating DINO features into a single CLS token obscures spatially distributed generation traces. To test this hypothesis, we introduce PatchHead, a lightweight spatial aggregation head that preserves the two-dimensional organization of DINO patch tokens and integrates evidence across neighboring regions. During training, we freeze the pretrained DINO backbone and optimize only the inserted LoRA adapters, PatchHead, and auxiliary projection head. Across nine cross-dataset benchmarks spanning manually curated and in-the-wild settings, PatchHead ranks first on seven datasets and second on the remaining two. It improves the strongest prior method from 91.6% to 94.6% in average balanced accuracy (+3.0 points) and raises the worst-case accuracy from 82.4% to 89.4% (+6.9 points), while introducing only 8.6% more trainable parameters and 0.08% additional FLOPs. Further qualitative analysis suggests that PatchHead (i) reduces class-conditional domain discrepancy, and (ii) redirects the representation from content-dominated saliency toward spatially distributed authenticity evidence. Together, these observations provide a representation-level account of why spatial patch aggregation transfers more reliably across generators and datasets than a single CLS-based global representation. Our code and models will be made available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [186] [RL-Native Distillation: Exploiting Scored Trajectories for Few-Step Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09226)
*Yuhan Li,Fangao Zeng,Sicong Kang,Mengfei Xu,Hao Zhou,Wei Li,Pipei Huang,Bingbing Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: REST是一个单阶段的RL-蒸馏联合训练框架，利用扩散RL过程中产生的奖励打分轨迹作为蒸馏监督源，通过优势调制蒸馏（AMD）将轨迹优势转换为带符号权重，实现少步数的无CFG推理，额外训练成本低于纯RL的25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方案中，RL奖励对齐和少步蒸馏是顺序执行的两阶段流程，导致训练成本增加，且压缩过程中可能丢失已获得的奖励增益。作者希望直接从扩散RL内部挖掘蒸馏监督信号，实现单阶段联合训练。

Method: 提出REST框架，将解耦的学生模型附加到任意RL教师模型上。学生从教师动态演化的rollout轨迹中分段学习，同时保持原始教师优化不变。引入Advantage-Modulated Distillation (AMD)机制，将rollout优势值转化为带符号权重作用于基础蒸馏损失，强化偏好轨迹的监督、轻微排斥低奖励轨迹。

Result: 在组合生成、视觉文本渲染和人类偏好对齐任务上，REST实现了少步数、无CFG的推理，性能匹配或超越其40步的RL教师。额外训练成本低于纯RL的25%。相比RTDMD，在DrawBench上的PickScore提升了0.82，且仅需要其五分之一的训练迭代次数。

Conclusion: REST证明了RL采样轨迹中的中间状态可以作为有效的蒸馏监督来源，而非采样过程中的副产物。该框架轻量、即插即用，无需额外图像rollout、独立蒸馏数据集或对抗训练，为RL与蒸馏的高效联合优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: Efficient text-to-image generation requires both reinforcement-learning (RL)-based reward alignment and few-step distillation, yet these procedures are typically performed sequentially, increasing training cost and risking the loss of reward gains during compression. We instead take an RL-native perspective: diffusion RL already generates reward-scored finite-step trajectories, whose intermediate states provide a natural source of distillation supervision rather than a disposable byproduct of sampling. Based on this insight, we propose REST (Reward-Enhanced Scored-Trajectory Distillation), a single-stage RL-distillation co-training framework that attaches a decoupled student to an arbitrary RL teacher. The student learns segment-wise from the teacher's evolving rollout trajectories while leaving the original teacher optimization unchanged. To prevent uniform imitation from preserving undesirable low-reward behaviors, we further introduce Advantage-Modulated Distillation (AMD), which transforms rollout advantages into signed weights over a base distillation loss. AMD strengthens supervision from preferred trajectories and mildly repels the student from low-reward ones. The resulting framework is lightweight and plug-and-play, requires no extra image rollouts, no separate distillation dataset, and no adversarial training. Experiments on compositional generation, visual text rendering, and human-preference alignment show that REST enables few-step CFG-free inference that matches or surpasses its 40-step RL teacher, with an overall additional training cost below 25% over pure RL. REST improves DrawBench PickScore over RTDMD by 0.82 while requiring only one-fifth of the training iterations.

</details>


### [187] [BAG: Budget-Aware Gating for Diffusion Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09231)
*Tong Zhao,Mingkun Lei,Yucheng Han,Chi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BAG（Budget-Aware Gating）是一种新型的扩散缓存策略，通过轻量级门控网络统一了全局预算控制和实例自适应特征复用，解决了现有扩散缓存方法在全局预算感知与实例自适应性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散缓存方法存在根本性权衡：在线启发式缺乏全局预算意识，而静态调度缺乏实例自适应性，无法灵活应对变化的运行时预算约束。

Method: 采用轻量级门控网络，动态决定每个去噪步骤是执行完整计算还是复用缓存特征，同时以预算状态和局部轨迹反馈为条件进行联合决策；通过离线到在线的调度蒸馏（offline-to-online schedule distillation）训练策略，将离线搜索的调度决策迁移到紧凑的在线门控中。

Result: 在FLUX.1-dev和Wan2.1上的大量实验表明，BAG在各种加速档位上始终优于最先进的缓存方法，并且在不同分辨率、随机种子和引导尺度下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BAG成功弥合了全局预算控制和实例自适应之间的鸿沟，为扩散变换器缓存提供了统一且自适应的解决方案，且代码将开源。

Abstract: Diffusion caching is a lightweight strategy that accelerates Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) by reusing intermediate features across denoising steps, but existing paradigms face a fundamental trade-off: online heuristics lack global budget awareness, whereas static schedules lack instance adaptivity and fail to flexibly adapt to varying runtime budget constraints. To bridge this gap, we present BAG (Budget-Aware Gating), a novel caching policy that unifies global budget pacing with dynamic, instance-adaptive feature reuse. Rather than relying on hand-crafted rules, BAG employs a lightweight gating network that dynamically decides whether to execute a full computation or reuse cached features at each step by jointly conditioning on the budget state and local trajectory feedback. We train this policy via offline-to-online schedule distillation, transferring the decision-making of offline-searched schedules into a compact online gate. Extensive experiments on FLUX.1-dev and Wan2.1 demonstrate that BAG consistently outperforms state-of-the-art caching methods across various speedup tiers while remaining robust across different resolutions, seeds, and guidance scales. Code will be released.

</details>


### [188] [RealDenseFace: Real-time Monocular 3D Face Reconstruction from Dense UV-space Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09238)
*Linzhou Li,Tianjia Shao,Kun Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealDenseFace是一种基于优化的实时3D人脸重建方法，利用密集UV空间网络预测，通过定制的高斯-牛顿求解器将3DMM拟合转化为非线性最小二乘问题，仅需数次迭代即可收敛，支持单图、离线序列重建和在线跟踪三种模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于优化的单目3D人脸重建方法虽然通过将3DMM拟合到网络预测的密集先验上取得了高保真度，但优化阶段计算开销极大，每张图像往往需要数十秒。这严重限制了此类方法在实际应用中的可用性，尤其是在实时场景下。

Method: 将3DMM拟合形式化为非线性最小二乘问题，并设计专用的高斯-牛顿求解器使其仅需少量迭代即可收敛。重建分两阶段：第一阶段由网络从单张RGB图像预测两个密集UV空间图（用于UV到图像对齐的对应图，以及用于沿视线方向几何约束的相对深度图）；第二阶段求解器在顶点UV坐标处采样目标并进行拟合。该方法支持单图拟合、离线序列重建和在线跟踪三种设置。

Result: 在NeRSemble SVFR基准上达到最先进精度；在线跟踪器运行速度超过80 FPS；离线序列重建速度比之前的优化方法快20倍以上。

Conclusion: RealDenseFace证明了基于优化的3D人脸重建方法在保持高精度（达到SOTA水平）的同时，也能实现实时性能，为优化类方法在实际应用中的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Recent monocular 3D face reconstruction methods achieve high fidelity by fitting a 3D Morphable Model (3DMM) to dense priors predicted by networks, but the optimization stage is computationally expensive, often taking tens of seconds per image. We present RealDenseFace, a real-time optimization-based 3D face reconstruction method with dense UV-space network predictions. Our key idea is to formulate 3DMM fitting as a nonlinear least-squares problem and solve it with a tailored Gauss-Newton solver that converges in only a few iterations. The reconstruction is conducted in two stages. In the first stage, the network predicts two dense UV-space maps from a single RGB image: a correspondence map for UV-to-image alignment, and a relative-depth map for geometric constraints along the viewing direction. In the second stage, the solver fits per-vertex targets sampled from these maps at the vertex UV coordinates. The solver supports all three reconstruction settings: single-image fitting, offline sequence reconstruction, and online tracking. Our method achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on the NeRSemble SVFR benchmark. The online tracker runs at 80+ FPS, and the offline sequence reconstruction is over 20 times faster than previous optimization-based baselines.

</details>


### [189] [In-Loop Model Adaptation with Coupled Latent-Noise Guidance for High-Fidelity Subject-Driven Text-to-Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09244)
*Yushun Tang,Weiming Chen,Siyi Liu,Yi Zhang,Feng Wu,Zhihai He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为In-Loop Model Adaptation (IMA)的新方法，用于主题驱动的文本到图像生成。与现有方法不同，IMA在图像生成过程中实时调整扩散模型，而无需在生成前用参考图像进行训练或微调，通过在DDIM反演链和文本到图像生成链之间引入掩码潜在一致性损失和噪声正则化损失来保持主题身份并确保文本对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题驱动的文本到图像生成中，当参考图像变化时，扩散模型难以在保持主题身份一致性的同时高效适应不同视觉上下文。现有方法要么需要大规模领域特定数据集进行训练，要么需要在生成前对参考图像进行数百次迭代的微调，这些方法效率低下且灵活性不足。

Method: IMA方法包含两条链：(1) DDIM反演链，将参考图像映射为潜在变量序列；(2) 文本到图像生成链，仅从文本提示生成图像。通过引入掩码潜在一致性损失和噪声正则化损失，刻画扩散模型与这两条链在每个生成步骤中的潜在-噪声差异，利用该耦合潜在-噪声损失指导生成过程中的模型实时适应，以在保持主题身份的同时与文本提示精确对齐。

Result: 大量实验表明，IMA方法显著提升了主题驱动的文本到图像生成性能，在保持参考图像主体身份的同时实现了与文本提示的准确对齐，生成了高保真度的图像。

Conclusion: IMA展示了在生成过程中实时适应扩散模型的可能性，无需预训练或微调，为高效的主题驱动文本到图像生成提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generating high-quality images from a given text prompt. Subject-driven generation aims to synthesize customized images to mimic the appearance of subjects in given reference images within different visual contexts specified by the text prompts. The central challenge here is that, when the reference image changes, the diffusion model cannot efficiently adapt to different visual contexts while consistently maintaining the subject identity. Existing methods either train the model with a large domain-specific dataset or fine-tune the model using the reference image for hundreds of iterations before actual image generation. In this work, we explore a new approach, called \textit{In-Loop Model Adaptation} (IMA), which adapts the core diffusion model at each generation step during the actual process of image generation, without being trained on the reference image before the generation process. To this end, we establish a DDIM inversion chain that maps the reference image to a sequence of latent, as well as a text-to-image generation chain which generates the image from the text prompt only. We then introduce a masked latent consistency loss and a noise regularization loss to characterize the latent-noise difference between the diffusion model and these two chains at each generation step. This coupled latent-noise loss is used to guide the in-loop model adaptation to preserve the subject identity specified by the reference image while maintaining accurate alignment with the text prompt, resulting in high-fidelity text-to-image generation. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed IMA method significantly improves the performance of subject-driven text-to-image generation.

</details>


### [190] [Task-Adaptive 3D Cross-Field MRI Translation via Field-Conditioned Content-Style Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09264)
*Haowen Pang,Yingqi Hao,Pengli Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于场条件内容-风格预训练的3D无配对跨场MRI翻译框架，用于解决MRIxFields 2026挑战中的跨磁场强度MRI翻译问题，涵盖0.1T到7T五个磁场强度的三个任务，通过解耦解剖内容与场依赖对比特征实现目标场图像生成并保持受试者解剖结构完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场强度是MRI中域偏移的主要来源，影响信噪比、组织对比度、空间细节和解剖边界可见性。跨场MRI翻译极具挑战性，因为同一受试者在多个场强下的配对采集数据很少可用于训练，需要解决无配对条件下的跨场翻译问题。

Method: 提出3D无配对跨场MRI翻译框架：(1) 通过解耦解剖内容与场依赖对比特征进行场条件内容-风格预训练，实现跨所有可用场强的可控翻译；(2) 将预训练骨干适配到任务特定目标域；(3) 模型包含3D内容编码器、3D风格编码器、场条件风格生成器、AdaIN调制解码器和多场判别器；(4) 采用对抗学习生成真实目标场外观，并通过循环一致性、身份、内容、风格和多样性约束保证解剖保真度和可控翻译。

Result: 在覆盖五个场强和三种MRI模态的MRIxFields数据上评估，配对测试数据的实验表明该框架能适应三种挑战设置，同时在合成体积中保持三维解剖结构。

Conclusion: 基于场条件内容-风格预训练的3D无配对翻译框架能有效解决跨磁场强度MRI翻译问题，适应任意到7T、0.1T到高场和任意到任意合成三种任务，同时保持解剖结构完整性。

Abstract: Magnetic field strength is a major source of domain shift in magnetic resonance imaging (MRI), affecting signal-to-noise ratio, tissue contrast, spatial detail, and the visibility of anatomical boundaries. The MRIxFields 2026 challenge investigates this problem through cross-field MRI translation across acquisitions at 0.1T, 1.5T, 3T, 5T, and 7T. Its three tasks, Any-to-7T, 0.1T-to-High, and Any-to-Any synthesis, require the generation of target-field image characteristics while preserving subject-specific anatomy. This problem is particularly challenging because paired acquisitions of the same subject across multiple field strengths are rarely available for training. We propose a 3D unpaired cross-field MRI translation framework based on field-conditioned content-style pretraining. The proposed framework first learns controllable field-to-field translation across all available field strengths by disentangling anatomical content from field-dependent contrast characteristics. The pretrained backbone is then adapted to task-specific target domains. Our model comprises a 3D content encoder, a 3D style encoder, a field-conditioned style generator, an AdaIN-modulated decoder, and a multi-field discriminator. Adversarial learning encourages realistic target-field appearance, while cycle-consistency, identity, content, style, and diversity constraints promote anatomical fidelity and controllable translation. We evaluate the proposed method on MRIxFields data spanning five field strengths and three MRI modalities. Experiments on paired test data demonstrate that the framework can adapt to the three challenge settings while preserving three-dimensional anatomical structure in the synthesized volumes. The implementation code is publicly available at https://github.com/Idea89560041/3D-MRI-Field-Translation.

</details>


### [191] [Did the Grid Erase the Event? EndoClock for Auditing Medical World-Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09266)
*Yarin Udi,Tom Sharon-Shahak,Roee Masad,Dan Pri-Tal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出四分类法（EndoClock）作为保守的预训练审计工具，用于检测医学世界模型在多模态数据同步到固定网格时，是否因内源性观测时钟而丢失任务相关证据。通过超声心动图案例展示了B-mode视频在脉冲波多普勒采集期间停止写入，仅外部采集日志保留测量事件的证据失败情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学世界模型通常将多模态录音同步到固定速率网格上作为预处理，这会将每个原生流重新采样到共享时间轴。当观测时钟依赖于潜在状态或采集状态（即内源性）时，同步可能并非中性的，会在模型看到数据之前就抹除任务相关的证据。

Method: 引入一个四制度分类法，刻画区分目标事件或状态所需证据的存活位置：采样值、网格单元更新模式、原生时间戳，或仅存在于外部采集通道中。EndoClock将该分类法转化为保守的预训练审计，报告由可用证据支持的最低证据承载表示，或在无法确定任何制度时报告为"未解决"。

Result: 在超声心动图中展示了该失败模式：B-mode视频写入在脉冲波多普勒采集期间停止，而对应的测量事件仅记录在外部采集日志中，说明了同步预处理如何抹除关键证据。

Conclusion: 这是一项初步的失败预警和可执行审计工具。实际信息是应保留原生观测过程足够长时间，以确定同步是否已擦除了目标任务所需的信息。

Abstract: Medical world models commonly learn from multimodal recordings synchronized onto a fixed-rate grid. This preprocessing resamples each native stream onto a shared time axis. Each stream has an observation clock that governs when observations are emitted or updated. When this clock depends on the latent or acquisition state, it is endogenous. In such settings, synchronization may not be neutral and can erase task-relevant evidence before the model sees the data. We introduce a four-regime taxonomy that characterizes where the evidence needed to distinguish a target event or state survives. The relevant witness may remain in the sampled values, in grid-cell update patterns, in native timing, or only in an external acquisition channel. EndoClock operationalizes this taxonomy as a conservative pretraining audit. It reports the lowest witness-bearing representation supported by the available evidence, or unresolved when no regime can be established. We illustrate this failure in echocardiography, where B-mode video write-outs cease during pulsed-wave Doppler acquisition while the corresponding measurement events remain recorded only in an external acquisition log. This work is a preliminary failure alert and executable audit. Its practical message is to preserve the native observation process long enough to determine whether synchronization has erased information required by the intended task.

</details>


### [192] [GRASP: Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype Learning for Fine-Grained Cross-Modal Understanding in Drone Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09270)
*Jiahui Cui,Yan Zhao,Kan Wei,Enze Zhu,Peirong Zhang,Lei Wang,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRASP学习框架，用于解决无人机视角下的细粒度跨模态视觉-语言理解问题。该框架通过区域聚焦对齐(RFA)和语义扰动增强匹配(SPEM)两种协同策略，分别应对宏观层面的跨模态焦点错位和微观层面的视觉同构问题，在GeoText-1652和ERA数据集上取得了竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机航拍场景中的细粒度跨模态理解对视觉-语言导航至关重要，但存在两大固有挑战：宏观层面，宽广视野和俯视角度使视觉表征中背景杂乱信息过多，导致跨模态焦点错位，模型倾向于关注全局环境相似性而忽略具体物体细节；微观层面，视觉同构现象造成歧义，即不同候选目标具有相似的几何结构但仅在细微属性上存在差异。这两大挑战严重制约了现有方法在航拍场景下的跨模态理解能力。

Method: 提出GRASP（Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype）学习框架，包含两个协同策略：(1) 区域聚焦对齐(RFA)，促进以物体为中心的跨模态对齐并抑制背景干扰；(2) 语义扰动增强匹配(SPEM)，利用前景纯化的语义原型码本(SPC)构造语义扰动负样本，实现细粒度语义判别。

Result: 在GeoText-1652基准和未见的ERA数据集上进行了大量实验，GRASP在无人机视角的细粒度图文检索中取得了竞争性性能，验证了其在航拍场景跨模态理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRASP通过双策略协同的方式有效解决了无人机视角下跨模态理解中的焦点错位和视觉同构问题，为航拍场景的细粒度图文检索提供了有效解决方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-grained cross-modal understanding in drone views is essential for aerial vision-language navigation. However, the inherent wide field of view and overhead perspective of drone scenarios impose dual challenges on vision-language understanding. At the macro level, overwhelming background clutter in visual representations leads to Cross-Modal Focus Misalignment, where the model prioritizes global environmental similarities over specific object details. At the micro level, Visual Isomorphism creates ambiguity, where candidates share similar geometric structures yet differ only in subtle attributes. To address these challenges, we propose the Granularity-Aware Region Alignment and Semantic Prototype (GRASP) learning framework, enhancing discriminative capability through two synergistic strategies. Specifically, we introduce Region-Focused Alignment (RFA) to promote object-centric cross-modal alignment while suppressing background interference. Concurrently, to tackle visual isomorphism, we propose Semantic Perturbation Enhanced Matching (SPEM), which leverages a foreground-purified Semantic Prototype Codebook (SPC) to construct semantically perturbed negatives for fine-grained semantic discrimination. Extensive experiments on the GeoText-1652 benchmark and the unseen ERA dataset demonstrate that GRASP achieves competitive performance in drone-view fine-grained image-text retrieval, validating its effectiveness for cross-modal understanding in aerial scenarios. Our code implementation is available at https://github.com/UCAS-JC/GRASP.

</details>


### [193] [UniDFKD: A Unified Semantic Prior Framework for Architecture-Agnostic Data-Free Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09287)
*Xuewan He,Tong Chu,Zihan Cheng,Yuchen Su,Qianxin Xia,Guoming Lu,Jielei Wang,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniDFKD是一个统一的数据无关知识蒸馏框架，用显式的、与架构无关的语义先验替代架构特定的统计先验（如BN统计），通过三个维度的设计（类别语义条件、空间语义锚定、空间语义蒸馏）分别控制"合成什么"、"证据在哪里"和"知识如何转移"，在CNN和ViT的同构与异构设置中均取得超过20%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DFKD方法严重依赖架构特定的统计先验（如Batch Normalization统计）来指导数据合成，但现代架构（如Vision Transformers）往往缺乏这些先验，导致合成数据语义质量下降，进而造成知识蒸馏性能的严重退化。

Method: UniDFKD从三个维度构建统一的合成-蒸馏流水线：(1) 类别语义条件（CSC）通过语言派生的嵌入持续调制生成器，定义"合成什么"以捕获语义多样性；(2) 空间语义锚定（SSA）将教师的空间归因锚定到高斯先验，决定"证据属于哪里"；(3) 空间语义蒸馏（SSD）显式对齐师生空间证据与预测，控制"知识如何转移"。

Result: 在CNN和ViT上的大量实验表明，UniDFKD在同构和异构设置下均建立了新的最先进水平，平均绝对性能提升超过20%。

Conclusion: 通过用架构无关的显式语义先验替代架构特定的统计先验，UniDFKD有效解决了现代架构下DFKD的性能退化问题，为统一的数据无关知识蒸馏提供了通用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-Free Knowledge Distillation (DFKD) transfers knowledge from a pretrained teacher model to a compact student model by synthesizing semantically informative data, eliminating the need for access to the original training dataset. Existing DFKD methods rely heavily on architecture-specific statistical priors (e.g., Batch Normalization statistics) to guide data synthesis, however, such architecture-dependent priors are often absent in modern architectures such as Vision Transformers (ViTs), resulting in degraded semantic quality of the synthesized data and consequently catastrophic performance degradation. In this paper, we propose \emph{UniDFKD}, a unified data-free knowledge distillation framework that replaces architecture-specific statistics with explicit, architecture-agnostic semantic priors. \emph{UniDFKD} governs the entire synthesis-distillation pipeline along three dimensions: (1) Categorical Semantic Conditioning (CSC) defines \emph{what} to synthesize by persistently modulating the generator with language-derived embeddings to capture semantic diversity; (2) Spatial Semantic Anchoring (SSA) dictates \emph{where} evidence belongs by anchoring the teacher's spatial attributions to a Gaussian prior; and (3) Spatial Semantic Distillation (SSD) controls \emph{how} knowledge is transferred by explicitly aligning teacher-student spatial evidence alongside predictions. Extensive experiments across CNNs and ViTs demonstrate that UniDFKD establishes a new state-of-the-art, outperforming existing methods by an average absolute margin of over 20\% in both homogeneous and heterogeneous settings.

</details>


### [194] [Bootstrapping Vision-Language Model for Hysteroscopic Surgical Scene Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09302)
*Jun Huang,Meiyi Chen,Zijie Yue,Yuhang Xiao,Fang Li,Hanli Wang,Xiaowen Tong,Yi Guo,Miaojing Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VLM-hyster，首个基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的宫腔镜手术场景分割方法，用于十五类宫腔镜手术场景中目标的像素级定位。方法利用预训练图像编码器结合Transformer解码器进行密集预测，并设计类别特定文本提示和掩码蒸馏分支以过滤低相关视觉特征。在包含4,020张高分辨率图像的多中心数据集上，VLM-hyster显著优于现有SOTA AI模型，并通过妇科医生评估、多中心及前瞻性验证证明了其鲁棒性和泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宫腔镜手术场景分割对于理解术中环境和计算机辅助干预至关重要，但该任务面临病灶间形态高度相似以及手术视频中镜面反射、运动模糊、液体遮挡等伪影的独特挑战，现有方法难以有效应对。

Method: VLM-hyster采用预训练图像编码器作为分割主干以提取稳健视觉特征，结合基于Transformer的解码器进行密集预测；设计类别特定文本提示，并引入掩码蒸馏分支过滤与文本提示相关性低的视觉特征，使模型聚焦于类别特定图像区域以提升分割性能。

Result: 在包含4,020张高分辨率图像及详细掩码标注的多中心宫腔镜手术场景数据集上，VLM-hyster显著优于现有最先进的AI模型；妇科医生的广泛评估以及多中心、前瞻性验证进一步证明了其鲁棒性和泛化性。

Conclusion: VLM-hyster在宫腔镜手术中展现了实现AI辅助器械和病灶定位的相当潜力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Hysteroscopic surgical scene segmentation plays a pivotal role in understanding the hysteroscopic intraoperative environment as well as computer-assisted intervention. However, this task presents unique challenges due to the high morphological similarity among different lesions and the presence of artifacts such as specular reflections, motion blur, and fluid occlusions in surgical videos. In this work, we propose the first vision-language model (VLM)-based hysteroscopic surgical scene segmentation method, which performs pixel-wise localization for fifteen representative categories in hysteroscopic surgical scenes. Our VLM-hyster has a segmentation backbone that utilizes the pretrained image encoder for robust visual feature extraction, coupled with a transformer-based decoder for dense prediction. Moreover, we design category-specific text prompts and incorporate a masked distillation branch to filter out visual features with low correlation to the text prompts, enabling the model to focus more effectively on category-specific image regions and thereby enhancing segmentation performance. We collect a large multicentric hysteroscopic surgical scene dataset, containing 4,020 high-resolution images with detailed mask annotations, for model training and evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that VLM-hyster substantially outperforms state-of-the-art AI models. Furthermore, extensive assessments by gynecologists, as well as multicentre and prospective validations, demonstrate VLM-hyster's robustness and generalizability. The results suggest that VLM-hyster earns considerable potential in enabling AI-assisted localization of surgical instruments and lesions in hysteroscopic surgeries. Code is available at https://github.com/viscom-tongji/VLM-hyster.

</details>


### [195] [Degraded Infrared Small Object Detection via Degradation-Adapted Physics-Guided Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09311)
*Xinkai Lu,Wenjun Chen,Yi Li,Yi Chang,Luxin Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DAISOD框架，通过识别退化类型与严重程度、自适应分支处理和物理引导恢复机制，在多种退化条件下实现鲁棒的红外小目标检测，并构建了覆盖多种退化类型和等级的退化红外小目标检测数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外小目标检测虽取得显著进展，但雾、非均匀性等退化会抑制目标与背景的对比度，显著增加检测难度。现有方法主要依赖图像复原作为预处理，但通常针对特定退化类型设计，难以泛化到不同退化场景。

Method: DAISOD包含三个核心步骤：首先识别退化的类型和严重程度，然后通过专用分支自适应地处理退化，最后融合各分支结果用于后续检测。此外，引入物理引导的恢复机制，显式估计退化参数并通过物理模型去除退化影响，避免过度恢复导致小目标被消除。同时构建了退化红外小目标检测数据集。

Result: 大量实验表明，DAISOD在多种退化条件下的检测性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: DAISOD通过退化感知的自适应处理与物理引导恢复相结合，有效解决了不同退化条件下红外小目标检测的泛化问题，验证了该框架的鲁棒性和优越性。

Abstract: Infrared small object detection has made significant progress in recent years. However, degradations such as fog and nonuniformity can suppress target-background contrast, substantially increasing detection difficulty. Existing methods mainly rely on image restoration as preprocessing, but they are typically designed for specific degradation types and fail to generalize to varying degradations. To alleviate this, we propose DAISOD, a degradation-adapted infrared small object detection framework for robust detection under different degradations. DAISOD first identifies the type and severity of degradations, then adapts the processing via dedicated branches, and finally fuses the results for subsequent detection. Moreover, a physics-guided restoration mechanism is incorporated to explicitly estimate degradation parameters and remove degradation effects through physical models, avoiding excessive restoration that may erase small targets. Moreover, we construct a degraded infrared small object detection dataset covering diverse degradation types and levels. Extensive experiments show that DAISOD outperforms state-of-the-art methods under various degradation conditions.

</details>


### [196] [MemeMind: Reference-Guided Trace Construction for Offline Context Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09316)
*Run Yang,Weihang Wang,Boheng Sheng,Yuchen He,Jielei Zhang,Pengyu Chen,Zhiyu Wu,Qiang Sun,Huyang Sun,Longwen Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MemeMind是一种离线上下文优化方法，利用参考答案为只有失败轨迹的查询组构建成功的工具使用示例，通过对动漫、漫画和游戏迷因的跨模态推理任务进行验证，在两个Qwen3-VL模型上分别比最强基线提升22.0%和21.1%（30B模型）及8.1%和8.0%（235B模型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线上下文优化通过修订指令和示例来改进智能体，但在适应集上的一些查询只产生失败的轨迹，导致优化器无法看到如何利用可用工具获得正确答案的成功示例，从而丢失关键经验信号。

Method: MemeMind包含两个核心组件：TraceBuilder利用离线参考答案识别所需证据，通过文本搜索、图像检索和视觉定位来构建工具轨迹，并在加入适应缓冲区前进行验证；ToolGuide将收集的轨迹总结为共享指南和各工具的独立指令。参考答案和构建的轨迹仅用于适应阶段，推理时使用冻结模型加学习到的指南。

Result: 在MemeX基准（1000个专家标注的迷因）上，MemeMind在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B上比最强上下文优化基线提升22.0%和21.1%，在Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B上提升8.1%和8.0%（GPT-5评判下，两个语言分区、两个独立评判员）。消融实验表明为失败组构建成功工具使用是最大的增益来源。

Conclusion: 为失败的查询组构建可验证的成功工具轨迹，能够显著增强离线上下文优化在复杂跨模态推理任务中的效果，且该策略在推理时带来更有效的证据获取能力。

Abstract: Offline context optimization improves an agent by revising its instructions and examples while keeping the model frozen. This approach learns from rollouts on an adaptation set, but some queries produce only failed rollouts. In these cases, the optimizer sees no successful example of how the available tools can reach the correct answer. We introduce MemeMind, which uses an offline reference answer to recover this missing experience. TraceBuilder identifies the evidence required by the reference, executes text search, image retrieval, and visual grounding, and verifies the resulting tool trace before adding it to the adaptation buffer. ToolGuide then summarizes the collected traces into a shared guide and separate instructions for each tool. The reference answers and constructed traces are used only during adaptation, while inference uses the learned guides with a frozen model. We study this problem through Anime, Comic, and Game meme interpretation. These memes combine edited and ambiguous visual content, overlaid text, long tail franchise knowledge, and culture specific references. Their interpretation can require coordinated visual grounding, image retrieval, and text search, making them a demanding setting in which native rollout groups may fail together. We evaluate MemeMind on MemeX, a benchmark of 1,000 such memes annotated by experts.
